+ "\"Home Alone awareness\" most commonly refers to safety guidelines for children or adults who are left unsupervised at home. For children, this involves assessing their maturity, establishing clear rules, and creating an emergency plan. For adults living alone, it means being aware of security risks and taking precautions to protect oneself."
+ "\"Home Alone awareness\" most commonly refers to safety guidelines for children or adults who are left unsupervised at home. For children, this involves assessing their maturity, establishing clear rules, and creating an emergency plan. For adults living alone, it means being aware of security risks and taking precautions to protect oneself.\n\n### For Children\nBefore leaving a child home alone, parents should assess their maturity, not just their age.\n\n### For Adults Living Alone\n**Key Safety Tips:**\n* **Maintain Privacy:** Avoid posting on social media that you are home alone.\n* **Enhance Security:** Keep all doors and windows locked. Consider a security system."

+ "{\n    \"private_search_result\": {\n        \"video_items\": [{\n            \"description\": \"Oakland A's general manager Billy Beane (Brad Pitt) challenges the system and defies conventional wisdom when he is forced to rebuild his small-market team on a limited budget.
